           mg/kg/day in both males and females over the course of 13 weeks, indicating systemic toxicity.<br>
           <br>- \*\*Decreased Spermatogenesis (Sperm Count, Production Rate, Motility)\*\*: Noted
           particularly in male rats treated with THFA dermal application. Decreases were observed at doses of
           300 and 1000 mg/kg/day for sperm count and production rate; motility decrease specifically noted at
           the higher dose.<br><br>These effects are pivotal as they directly influenced NOAEL determination,
           highlighting systemic toxicity thresholds:<br><br>- \*\*NOAEL Determination\*\*: Identified to be
           100 mg/kg/day for male rats based on decreased spermatogenesis and lower body weight metrics. For
           female rats, it was determined to be 300 mg/kg/day after considering the adverse outcomes of
           reduced mean body weights.<br>
